<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01LxeKCKEbUnQT8V2hTYfb9p`</summary>

```text
{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"# PACS Profiling — full MVP pipeline (v4: importance-based masking).\\n#\\n# End-to-end:\\n#   1. Load causal LM + MATH-500 (stratified 50 problems)\\n#   2. Generate reasoning traces, segment into phases (quantile-based)\\n#   3. Per problem: dense forward with output_attentions to get CE losses\\n#      + head importance (Frobenius norm) + KV importance (cumulative attention)\\n#   4. \\\"reasoning\\\" / \\\"execution\\\" / \\\"transition\\\"\\n    start: int           # token start position (inclusive)\\n    end: int             # token end position (exclusive)\\n    mean_sparsity: float\\n\\n    @property\\n    def token_indices(self) -> List[int]:\\n        return list(range(self.start, self.end))\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass PhaseAnnotation:\\n    phases: List[Phase]\\n    boundaries: List[int]       # token indices where phase transitions occur\\n    sparsity: np.ndarray        # raw per-token sparsity, shape (seq_len,)\\n    kde_x: np.ndarray\\n    kde_density: np.ndarray\\n    metadata: dict = field(default_factory=dict)\\n\\n\\nclass PhaseSegmenter:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Segment a token sequence into reasoning / transition / execution phases\\n    based on the attention sparsity profile and Gaussian KDE peak detection.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(\\n        self,\\n        bandwidth: str = \\\"scott\\\",\\n        min_phase_length: int = 10,\\n        sparsity_threshold_ratio: float = 0.01,\\n        kde_grid_points: int = 512,\\n        min_seq_length: int = 50,\\n    ):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Args:\\n            bandwidth: KDE bandwidth selection — \\\"scott\\\", \\\"silverman\\\", or a float.\\n            min_phase_length: Minimum tokens per phase; shorter segments are merged\\n                into their left neighbour.\\n            sparsity_threshold_ratio: Fraction of row-wise max used as the sparsity\\n                cutoff (default 1 %).\\n            kde_grid_points: Resolution of the KDE evaluation grid.\\n            min_seq_length: Sequences shorter than this are returned as a single phase.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        self.bandwidth = bandwidth\\n        self.min_phase_length = min_phase_length\\n        self.sparsity_threshold_ratio = sparsity_threshold_ratio\\n        self.kde_grid_points = kde_grid_points\\n        self.min_seq_length = min_seq_length\\n\\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\\n    # Step 1: per-token attention sparsity\\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\n    def compute_attention_sparsity(\\n        self, attention_weights: torch.Tensor\\n    ) -> np.ndarray:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute per-token sparsity averaged over heads.\\n\\n        For each head and each query position, sparsity is the fraction of\\n        attention scores below ``row_max * sparsity_threshold_ratio``.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            attention_weights: shape ``(num_heads, seq_len, seq_len)``.\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            Per-token sparsity array of shape ``(seq_len,)``.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        # (H, S, S)\\n        attn = attention_weights.detach().float()\\n        if attn.dim() == 4:\\n            # (B, H, S, S) -> take first batch element\\n            attn = attn[0]\\n        assert attn.dim() == 3, f\\\"Expected 3-D attention tensor, got shape {attn.shape}\\\"\\n\\n        # Row-wise max per head per query position: (H, S, 1)\\n        row_max, _ = attn.max(dim=-1, keepdim=True)\\n        threshold = row_max * self.sparsity_threshold_ratio\\n\\n        # Fraction of keys below threshold for each (head, query) pair\\n        sparse_mask = (attn < threshold).float()         # (H, S, S)\\n        per_head_sparsity = sparse_mask.mean(dim=-1)     # (H, S)\\n\\n        # Average across heads -> (S,)\\n    \\n\\n... returning single phase.\\\",\\n                seq_len, self.min_seq_length,\\n            )\\n            phase = Phase(\\n                name=\\\"execution\\\",\\n                start=0,\\n                end=seq_len,\\n                mean_sparsity=float(sparsity.mean()),\\n            )\\n            dummy_x = np.linspace(0, 1, 2)\\n            return PhaseAnnotation(\\n                phases=[phase],\\n                boundaries=[],\\n                sparsity=sparsity,\\n                kde_x=dummy_x,\\n                kde_density=np.zeros(2),\\n                metadata={\\\"short_sequence\\\": True, \\\"bandwidth\\\": self.bandwidth},\\n            )\\n\\n        kde_x, kde_density = self.fit_kde(sparsity)\\n\\n        meta: dict = {\\\"bandwidth\\\": self.bandwidth}\\n        boundary_indices = self.find_phase_boundaries(\\n            kde_density, kde_x, sparsity=sparsity, _meta=meta,\\n        )\\n\\n        phases = self._assign_phases(sparsity, kde_x, boundary_indices)\\n        token_boundaries = [p.start for p in phases[1:]]\\n\\n        return PhaseAnnotation(\\n            phases=phases,\\n            boundaries=token_boundaries,\\n            sparsity=sparsity,\\n            kde_x=kde_x,\\n            kde_density=kde_density,\\n            metadata=meta,\\n        )\\n\\n    def segment_from_sparsity(self, sparsity: np.ndarray) -> PhaseAnnotation:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Segment from a pre-computed per-token sparsity sequence.\\n\\n        Used when sparsity is computed incrementally (e.g., per generation step)\\n        rather than from a full attention matrix.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        seq_len = len(sparsity)\\n\\n        if seq_len < self.min_seq_length:\\n            logger.info(\\n                \\\"Sequence length %d < %d; returning single phase.\\\",\\n                seq_len, self.min_seq_length,\\n            )\\n            phase = Phase(\\n                name=\\\"execution\\\",\\n                start=0,\\n                end=seq_len,\\n                mean_sparsity=float(sparsity.mean()),\\n            )\\n            dummy_x = np.linspace(0, 1, 2)\\n            return PhaseAnnotation(\\n                phases=[phase],\\n                boundaries=[],\\n                sparsity=sparsity,\\n                kde_x=dummy_x,\\n                kde_density=np.zeros(2),\\n                metadata={\\\"short_sequence\\\": True, \\\"bandwidth\\\": self.bandwidth},\\n            )\\n\\n        kde_x, kde_density = self.fit_kde(sparsity)\\n\\n        meta: dict = {\\\"bandwidth\\\": self.bandwidth}\\n        boundary_indices = self.find_phase_boundaries(\\n            kde_density, kde_x, sparsity=sparsity, _meta=meta,\\n        )\\n\\n        phases = self._assign_phases(sparsity, kde_x, boundary_indices)\\n        token_boundaries = [p.start for p in phases[1:]]\\n\\n        return PhaseAnnotation(\\n            phases=phases,\\n            boundaries=token_boundaries,\\n            sparsity=sparsity,\\n            kde_x=kde_x,\\n            kde_density=kde_density,\\n            metadata=meta,\\n        )\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.51}}"}

For each sparsity level (10%, 20%):\\n#      - Build importance-based masks (prune least important heads/KV positions)\\n#      - Compute per-phase perplexity under 4 conditions (dense/attn/kv/both)\\n#      - Compute per-phase Interaction Index\\n#   5. Bootstrap 95% CI per (phase, level)\\n#   6. Pass/fail judgment (CI overlap + sign reversal, OR logic)\\n#   7. Output structured JSON\\n#\\n# Usage:\\n#   python -m pacs.profiling.run_profiling \\\\\\n#     --model_path &lt;HF model ID or path> \\\\\\n#     --data_path &lt;MATH-500 JSON> \\\\\\n#     --output_dir ./profiling_output\\n\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\nfrom collections import defaultdict\\nfrom itertools import combinations\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nfrom scipy import stats as scipy_stats\\nimport torch\\n\\nfrom ..utils.data_utils import stratified_sample_math500\\nfrom ..utils.metrics import compute_perplexity, ppl_from_cached_losses\\nfrom .interaction_index import InteractionIndexCalculator, _HeadMaskHook, bootstrap_interaction_index\\nfrom .phase_segmentation import PhaseSegmenter\\nfrom .alt_segmentation import segment_by_quantile, to_phase_annotation\\n\\nlogging.basicConfig(\\n    level=logging.INFO,\\n    format=\\\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(name)s: %(message)s\\\",\\n)\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\\\"run_profiling\\\")\\n\\nDEFAULT_SPARSITY_LEVELS = [0.1, 0.2, 0.3]\\nN_BOOTSTRAP = 1000\\nMIN_SEGMENTS_WARN = 20\\nSEED = 42\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Model / data loading\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef load_model_and_tokenizer(model_path: str, device: str = \\\"cuda\\\"):\\n    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\\n\\n    logger.info(\\\"Loading tokenizer from %s\\\", model_path)\\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_path, trust_remote_code=True)\\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\\n\\n    logger.info(\\\"Loading model from %s\\\", model_path)\\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\\n        model_path,\\n        torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\\n        device_map=device,\\n        trust_remote_code=True,\\n        attn_implementation=\\\"eager\\\",\\n    )\\n    model.eval()\\n    return model, tokenizer\\n\\n\\ndef load_data(data_path: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\\n    logger.info(\\\"Loading data from %s\\\", data_path)\\n    with open(data_path) as f:\\n        data = json.load(f)\\n    if isinstance(data, dict) and \\\"data\\\" in data:\\n        data = data[\\\"data\\\"]\\n    logger.info(\\\"Loaded %d problems\\\", len(data))\\n    return data\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Phase-conditioned perplexity\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef compute_conditioned_perplexities(\\n    ii_calc: InteractionIndexCalculator,\\n    input_ids: torch.Tensor,\\n    phase_token_indices: List[int],\\n    attn_head_mask: torch.Tensor,\\n    kv_mask: torch.Tensor,\\n    dense_ce_losses: torch.Tensor,\\n    seq_len: int,\\n) -> Dict[str, float]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Run 3 sparse forward passes; dense ppl from cached CE losses.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    ppl_dense = ppl_from_cached_losses(dense_ce_losses, phase_token_indices, seq_len)\\n\\n    ppl_attn = ii_calc.forward_with_sparse_config(\\n        input_ids, attn_head_mask=attn_head_mask,\\n        phase_token_indices=phase_token_indices,\\n    )\\n    ppl_kv = ii_calc.forward_with_sparse_config(\\n        input_ids, kv_mask=kv_mask,\\n        phase_token_indices=phase_token_indices,\\n    )\\n    ppl_both = ii_calc.forward_with_sparse_config(\\n        input_id\\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 2889 字符] ...\\n\\n\\n\\n    raw_data = load_data(args.data_path)\\n    data = stratified_sample_math500(raw_data, seed=SEED)\\n    if args.max_problems is not None:\\n        data = data[:args.max_problems]\\n    logger.info(\\\"Stratified sample: %d problems\\\", len(data))\\n\\n    segmenter = PhaseSegmenter(\\n        bandwidth=args.kde_bandwidth,\\n        min_phase_length=args.min_phase_length,\\n    )\\n    ii_calc = InteractionIndexCalculator(model, tokenizer, device=device)\\n\\n    os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)\\n    warnings_list: List[str] = []\\n\\n    # Accumulators: {phase_name: {level_str: [ii_values]}}\\n    ii_per_phase_level: Dict[str, Dict[str, List[float]]] = defaultdict(\\n        lambda: defaultdict(list)\\n    )\\n    # Accumulators: {phase_name: {level_str: {condition: [ppl_values]}}}\\n    ppl_per_phase_level: Dict[str, Dict[str, Dict[str, List[float]]]] = defaultdict(\\n        lambda: defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(list))\\n    )\\n    # Phase token counts\\n    phase_token_counts: Dict[str, List[int]] = defaultdict(list)\\n    phase_segment_counts: Dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)\\n\\n    # P1 diagnostics accumulators\\n    all_problem_diagnostics: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\\n    all_sparsity_values: List[float] = []\\n\\n    # Per-problem II accumulator (keyed by level_str)\\n    per_problem_ii_data: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]] = defaultdict(list)\\n\\n    # Locate attention layers once for hook registration (C1/C3 fix)\\n    attn_layers = _HeadMaskHook._find_attention_layers(model)\\n    if not attn_layers:\\n        raise RuntimeError(\\\"Could not find attention layers in model\\\")\\n    last_attn_layer = attn_layers[-1]\\n\\n    for idx, item in enumerate(data):\\n        problem_text = item.get(\\\"problem\\\", item.get(\\\"question\\\", \\\"\\\"))\\n        problem_id = item.get(\\\"id\\\", str(idx))\\n        logger.info(\\\"[%d/%d] Processing problem %s\\\", idx + 1, len(data), problem_id)\\n\\n        prompt = (\\n            f\\\"Solve the following math problem step by step.\\\\n\\\\n\\\"\\n            f\\\"Problem: {problem_text}\\\\n\\\\nSolution:\\\"\\n        )\\n        inputs = tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\").to(device)\\n        prompt_len = inputs[\\\"input_ids\\\"].shape[1]\\n\\n        # --- C1/C3 fix: hook-based per-step sparsity, no output_attentions accumulation ---\\n        step_sparsities: List[float] = []\\n\\n        def _enable_attn_pre_hook(module, args, kwargs):\\n            kwargs = dict(kwargs)\\n            kwargs[\\\"output_attentions\\\"] = True\\n            return args, kwargs\\n\\n        def _capture_sparsity_hook(module, input, output):\\n            if isinstance(output, tuple) and len(output) > 1 and output[1] is not None:\\n                attn_w = output[1]\\n                if attn_w.shape[-2] == 1:\\n                    step_sparsities.append(\\n                        segmenter.compute_step_sparsity(attn_w[0])\\n                    )\\n                return (output[0],) + (None,) + output[2:]\\n\\n        pre_h = last_attn_layer.register_forward_pre_hook(\\n            _enable_attn_pre_hook, with_kwargs=True\\n        )\\n        post_h = last_attn_layer.register_forward_hook(_capture_sparsity_hook)\\n\\n        try:\\n            outputs = model.generate(\\n                **inputs,\\n                max_new_tokens=args.max_new_tokens,\\n                do_sample=False,\\n            )\\n        finally:\\n            pre_h.remove()\\n            post_h.remove()\\n\\n        full_ids = outputs.sequences if hasattr(outputs, \\\"sequences\\\") else outputs\\n        if full_ids.dim() == 1:\\n            full_ids = full_ids.unsqueeze(0)\\n\\n        if len(step_sparsities) == 0:\\n            logger.warning(\\\"No generation steps for problem %s, skipping.\\\", problem_id)\\n            warnings_list.append(f\\\"No generation steps for {problem_id}\\\")\\n            continue\\n\\n        # Phase segmentation on the generation trace (quantile-based)\\n        gen_sparsity = np.array(step_sparsities)\\n        quant_seg = segment_by_quantile(gen_sparsity, n_groups=3)\\n        annotation = to_phase_annotation(\\n            quant_seg.assignments,\\n            quant_seg.group_names,\\n            gen_sparsity,\\n            offset=0,\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.45}}"}

---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef load_model_and_tokenizer(model_path: str, device: str = \\\"cuda\\\"):\\n    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\\n\\n    logger.info(\\\"Loading tokenizer from %s\\\", model_path)\\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_path, trust_remote_code=True)\\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\\n\\n    logger.info(\\\"Loading model from %s\\\", model_path)\\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\\n        model_path,\\n        torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\\n        device_map=device,\\n        trust_remote_code=True,\\n        attn_implementation=\\\"eager\\\",\\n    )\\n    model.eval()\\n    return model, tokenizer\\n\\n\\ndef load_data(data_path: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\\n    logger.info(\\\"Loading data from %s\\\", data_path)\\n    with open(data_path) as f:\\n        data = json.load(f)\\n    if isinstance(data, dict) and \\\"data\\\" in data:\\n        data = data[\\\"data\\\"]\\n    logger.info(\\\"Loaded %d problems\\\", len(data))\\n    return data\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Phase-conditioned perplexity\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef compute_conditioned_perplexities(\\n    ii_calc: InteractionIndexCalculator,\\n    input_ids: torch.Tensor,\\n    phase_token_indices: List[int],\\n    attn_head_mask: torch.Tensor,\\n    kv_mask: torch.Tensor,\\n    dense_ce_losses: torch.Tensor,\\n    seq_len: int,\\n) -> Dict[str, float]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Run 3 sparse forward passes; dense ppl from cached CE losses.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    ppl_dense = ppl_from_cached_losses(dense_ce_losses, phase_token_indices, seq_len)\\n\\n    ppl_attn = ii_calc.forward_with_sparse_config(\\n        input_ids, attn_head_mask=attn_head_mask,\\n        phase_token_indices=phase_token_indices,\\n    )\\n    ppl_kv = ii_calc.forward_with_sparse_config(\\n        input_ids, kv_mask=kv_mask,\\n        phase_token_indices=phase_token_indices,\\n    )\\n    ppl_both = ii_calc.forward_with_sparse_config(\\n        input_id\\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 2889 字符] ...\\n\\n\\n\\n    raw_data = load_data(args.data_path)\\n    data = stratified_sample_math500(raw_data, seed=SEED)\\n    if args.max_problems is not None:\\n        data = data[:args.max_problems]\\n    logger.info(\\\"Stratified sample: %d problems\\\", len(data))\\n\\n    segmenter = PhaseSegmenter(\\n        bandwidth=args.kde_bandwidth,\\n        min_phase_length=args.min_phase_length,\\n    )\\n    ii_calc = InteractionIndexCalculator(model, tokenizer, device=device)\\n\\n    os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)\\n    warnings_list: List[str] = []\\n\\n    # Accumulators: {phase_name: {level_str: [ii_values]}}\\n    ii_per_phase_level: Dict[str, Dict[str, List[float]]] = defaultdict(\\n        lambda: defaultdict(list)\\n    )\\n    # Accumulators: {phase_name: {level_str: {condition: [ppl_values]}}}\\n    ppl_per_phase_level: Dict[str, Dict[str, Dict[str, List[float]]]] = defaultdict(\\n        lambda: defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(list))\\n    )\\n    # Phase token counts\\n    phase_token_counts: Dict[str, List[int]] = defaultdict(list)\\n    phase_segment_counts: Dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)\\n\\n    # P1 diagnostics accumulators\\n    all_problem_diagnostics: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\\n    all_sparsity_values: List[float] = []\\n\\n    # Per-problem II accumulator (keyed by level_str)\\n    per_problem_ii_data: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]] = defaultdict(list)\\n\\n    # Locate attention layers once for hook registration (C1/C3 fix)\\n    attn_layers = _HeadMaskHook._find_attention_layers(model)\\n    if not attn_layers:\\n        raise RuntimeError(\\\"Could not find attention layers in model\\\")\\n    last_attn_layer = attn_layers[-1]\\n\\n    for idx, item in enumerate(data):\\n        problem_text = item.get(\\\"problem\\\", item.get(\\\"question\\\", \\\"\\\"))\\n        problem_id = item.get(\\\"id\\\", str(idx))\\n        logger.info(\\\"[%d/%d] Processing problem %s\\\", idx + 1, len(data), problem_id)\\n\\n        prompt = (\\n            f\\\"Solve the following math problem step by step.\\\\n\\\\n\\\"\\n            f\\\"Problem: {problem_text}\\\\n\\\\nSolution:\\\"\\n        )\\n        inputs = tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\").to(device)\\n        prompt_len = inputs[\\\"input_ids\\\"].shape[1]\\n\\n        # --- C1/C3 fix: hook-based per-step sparsity, no output_attentions accumulation ---\\n        step_sparsities: List[float] = []\\n\\n        def _enable_attn_pre_hook(module, args, kwargs):\\n            kwargs = dict(kwargs)\\n            kwargs[\\\"output_attentions\\\"] = True\\n            return args, kwargs\\n\\n        def _capture_sparsity_hook(module, input, output):\\n            if isinstance(output, tuple) and len(output) > 1 and output[1] is not None:\\n                attn_w = output[1]\\n                if attn_w.shape[-2] == 1:\\n                    step_sparsities.append(\\n                        segmenter.compute_step_sparsity(attn_w[0])\\n                    )\\n                return (output[0],) + (None,) + output[2:]\\n\\n        pre_h = last_attn_layer.register_forward_pre_hook(\\n            _enable_attn_pre_hook, with_kwargs=True\\n        )\\n        post_h = last_attn_layer.register_forward_hook(_capture_sparsity_hook)\\n\\n        try:\\n            outputs = model.generate(\\n                **inputs,\\n                max_new_tokens=args.max_new_tokens,\\n                do_sample=False,\\n            )\\n        finally:\\n            pre_h.remove()\\n            post_h.remove()\\n\\n        full_ids = outputs.sequences if hasattr(outputs, \\\"sequences\\\") else outputs\\n        if full_ids.dim() == 1:\\n            full_ids = full_ids.unsqueeze(0)\\n\\n        if len(step_sparsities) == 0:\\n            logger.warning(\\\"No generation steps for problem %s, skipping.\\\", problem_id)\\n            warnings_list.append(f\\\"No generation steps for {problem_id}\\\")\\n            continue\\n\\n        # Phase segmentation on the generation trace (quantile-based)\\n        gen_sparsity = np.array(step_sparsities)\\n        quant_seg = segment_by_quantile(gen_sparsity, n_groups=3)\\n        annotation = to_phase_annotation(\\n            quant_seg.assignments,\\n            quant_seg.group_names,\\n            gen_sparsity,\\n            offset=0,\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.45}}"

## Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 干跑验证结果

### 1. 模型完整性 ✓
- 4 个 safetensors 分片（共 ~14.3GB），config.json / tokenizer_config.json / generation_config.json 齐全

### 2. 代码兼容性 ✓ — 无需修改
- `run_profiling.py` 及整个 `pacs/profiling/` 目录中 **零** DeepSeek-specific 逻辑（无 "DeepSeek"、"deepseek" 字符串）
- `model_path` 是 CLI 参数 `--model_path`，非 hardcode
- `load_model_and_tokenizer()` 使用 `AutoModelForCausalLM` + `AutoTokenizer`，完全通用
- prompt 构建是纯文本模板 `"Solve the following math problem step by step.\n\nProblem: ...\n\nSolution:"`，不依赖 chat template
- `pad_token` 自动 fallback 到 `eos_token`（Qwen2.5 自带 pad_token `<|endoftext|>`，不会触发 fallback）
- bf16 已配置（line 71: `torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16`）

### 3. Thinking Tag 兼容性 ✓ — 不相关
- profiling 代码中 **零** thinking tag 处理。phase segmentation 完全基于 **attention sparsity quantile**（非文本标记）
- Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 不是 reasoning 模型，不会生成 `&lt;think>` tag（那是 QwQ/Qwen3 才有的）
- DeepSeek-R1-Distill 虽然会生成 `&lt;think>` tag，但代码也没有用它做分割——所以两个模型在这方面完全一致

### 4. 架构对比

| 参数 | DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B | Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct |
|------|------|------|
| architecture | Qwen2ForCausalLM | Qwen2ForCausalLM |
| num_hidden_layers | 28 | 28 |
| num_attention_heads | 28 | 28 |
| num_key_value_heads | 4 | 4 |
| hidden_size | 3584 | 3584 |
| intermediate_size | 18944 | 18944 |
| vocab_size | 152064 | 152064 |
| rope_theta | 10000 | 1000000 |
| max_position_embeddings | 131072 | 32768 |

架构完全相同（同一 base model 微调），仅 rope_theta 和 max_position_embeddings 不同（不影响 profiling）。

### 5. 预估修改：零修改

### 6. 建议的 Qwen 实验命令

```bash
cd /root/autodl-tmp/pacs && CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=X python -m pacs.profiling.run_profiling \
    --model_path /root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct \
    --data_path /root/autodl-tmp/pacs/data/math500.json \
    --output_dir ./profiling_output/qwen25_7b_v6_importance \
    --masking_method importance
```

替换 `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=X` 为空闲 GPU 即可，无需额外参数。`--masking_method random` 同理。
